+
+## Method and independently checked claims
+
+Repair notes were treated as hypotheses. Historical **FIXED / NOT FIXED** labels below apply to each exact reproduction, not every spelling of its defect class. Earlier scripts were reused with provenance and output-path adaptations; M2's partial work and completed M2b are included. A newly introduced refusal can close a reproduction without repairing its underlying isotope parser, and those cases are explicitly distinguished.
+
+- **350 tests passed in 43.12 s**, exit 0. The requested pytest command was run with bytecode disabled and `--basetemp` inside this round. [Output](contract/pytest.txt), [command/environment](contract/pytest_summary.json).
+- **6,469 live centres / 22 relation-direction-type-role combinations / zero independent-oracle errors**. **19 DB assertions, 24 UI assertions and four additional self-loop/unknown-type/retirement checks** pass. [Oracle](contract/db_validate.json), [DB assertions](contract/db_assertions.json), [UI assertions](contract/ui_assertions.json), [extra fixtures](contract/self_loop_unknown.json).
+- **36/36 ordinary demos** agree between direct Engine and original CLI HTTP; actual JavaScript callbacks pass the 36 examples and six map examples. [HTTP](contract/demo_http.json), [prior comparison](contract/demo_previous_comparison.json), [callbacks](contract/ui_m3_checks.json).
+- The new attack suite has **87 current/HEAD^ case entries** plus **four supplemental negation entries** (overlapping inputs are not summed as unique cases). Its **348 original CLI HTTP requests all return 200**. Assertions independently establish **17 leaking variants**, including complete map equality and both complete QA evidence-array equalities with the corresponding bare alias; **30 refusal controls** have no centre and zero QA evidence. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [negation supplement](attacks/negation_results.json).
+- The actual form callback renders all **87 HTTP map captures** without render errors. The blocker inputs produce a parent SVG and `CHEMBL1201585` trail. [Page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+The page harness runs the actual JavaScript in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. It tests generated markup, callbacks and state, not browser painting or browser-executed XSS. HEAD^ comparisons replace only `query.py` against the current other modules/data, not an entire historical deployment. Cost numbers are host observations. No model was called.
+
+## M23-B1 — symbol-first isotope inside a larger word still draws the parent (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `DOTACe134 trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab DOTAMn52`.** Both return **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, with **13 map trials**, **12 trial QA rows** and **12 sponsor QA rows**. Each complete HTTP map and each complete evidence array equals the appropriate bare-trastuzumab result. Reversing the phrase, adding a trailing chelator (`DOTACe134DOTA trastuzumab`), using `DOTAMn52m`, and using Herceptin corroborate the same mechanism. [All-stage traces](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page rendering](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+Paired controls **`DOTA-Ce134 trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA134Ce trastuzumab`**, **`Ce134DOTA trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA52Mn trastuzumab`** and **`DOTA-Ce-134 trastuzumab`** all refuse. The issue is specifically the position of a symbol-first pair inside a word; the exact M22 mass-first cases are fixed.
+
+**Cause:** the new symbol-first expression at `src/query.py:268` is `(?<![a-z])([a-z]{2})(\d{1,3})(?!\d)`. Its negative lookbehind rejects the `Ce134` part of `dotace134` because another letter precedes it. `Ce134DOTA` matches at the start. The corresponding mass-first expression at `src/query.py:264-267` does allow preceding letters. Ce/Mn exist in `_NAME_MASS` but not the older `_MEDICAL_MASS` substring scanner (`src/query.py:124-136,144-158`); the neighbour/edge fallback uses an element set that also lacks them (`src/textrules.py:290-294`). Thus no later stage rescues the missed pair. The audit records the regex matches and actual stage outcomes, rather than inferring the failure solely from the source. [Assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [independent second probe](history/spec_probe.json).
+
+**Identity evidence:** Bailey et al. prepared and imaged `[134Ce]Ce-DOTA-Trastuzumab`. The compact audit input `DOTACe134` is a reviewer-generated notation variant of that labelled material, **not** a verbatim published name. The primary study supports the distinction from unmodified trastuzumab; it does not justify attaching the originator's graph evidence to the conjugate. [Primary study, DOI 10.1016/j.nucmedbio.2022.04.007](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0969805122000373).
+
+Required result: refuse the modified-product description or resolve the correct distinct product. A regression check should pair symbol-first **start / interior / end** positions with mass-first controls and the documented ordinary-text exceptions. No application repair was made in this audit.
+
+## M23-B2 — explicit nonradioactive conjugates still inherit the originator graph (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `trastuzumab conjugated to IRDye800CW` and `trastuzumab conjugated to DM1`.** Both pass every refusal stage and produce the complete bare-trastuzumab map, **13 map trials / 12 trial QA rows / 12 sponsor QA rows**, over original CLI HTTP and actual page callbacks. `IRDye800CW-conjugated trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab IRDye800CW conjugate`, `trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1`, `trastuzumab DM1 conjugate` and Herceptin variants corroborate the same missing product-descriptor guard. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [full-equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+This groups the optical-dye and cytotoxic-payload examples as **one descriptor-coverage mechanism**, not two independently counted code defects. M22 already recorded related IRDye/DM1/ADC strings as unadjudicated diagnostics. This round supplies explicit conjugation inputs and primary identity evidence; these were never user-accepted exceptions.
+
+**Identity evidence:** Jeong et al. describe covalently constructing a trastuzumab–IRDye800CW optical imaging probe and evaluating the resulting conjugate. The query explicitly names that modification. [Primary study, DOI 10.5230/jgc.2021.21.e18](https://jgc-online.org/DOIx.php?id=10.5230%2Fjgc.2021.21.e18). The FDA's KADCYLA description identifies trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1 via MCC as an antibody-drug conjugate, and its dosing section explicitly distinguishes it from trastuzumab. A phrase saying only “conjugated to DM1” need not uniquely identify that approved linker/product, but it still cannot designate the unconjugated parent. [FDA prescribing information, sections 11 and 2.2](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/label/2026/125427s125lbl.pdf). These are identity premises, not treatment recommendations or claims of clinical benefit. [Source notes and limits](attacks/sources.json).
+
+**Cause:** `RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` covers biosimilar/reformulation descriptors but no conjugated/covalently-linked payload construction (`src/textrules.py:139-152,202-212`). `_qualified` delegates to those product/tracer predicates (`src/query.py:315-337`). The new label check recognizes the enumerated radioactive stems and exact words `labelled`, `labeled`, `labelling`, `labeling`, `tagged`, but not these nonradioactive conjugation descriptions (`src/query.py:178-199,227-236`). The resolver yields CONTAINED_ALIAS plus UNRESOLVED residue; `_resolver_blocked` remains empty, `_marked` returns None, and the parent is emitted at `src/query.py:662-679`.
+
+Controls `IRDye800CW-labelled trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab tagged with IRDye800CW` correctly refuse. Merely replacing “labelled” by the equally explicit “conjugated to” loses the protection. `trastuzumab-DM1`, `ado-trastuzumab emtansine` and `Kadcyla` do **not** draw the parent in the exercised standalone calls, so they are not counted as leaking controls. The latter two are ambiguous in the current dictionary. No automatic choice of a specific ADC is required to fix this failure: refusal is sufficient.
+

+
+For each principal blocker input, the actual current calls show:
+

+|---|---|

+
+The map selects the accepted hit at `src/app.py:88-95`. This is **not** a case where the map discards an existing Refused entity; every guard misses the qualification. Both blockers are inherited with HEAD^ and independently fail the original product-identity approval bar.
+
+## Repair controls and should-fix observations
+
+The new mass-first Mn parser recognizes `52Mn` and `52mMn`; compact mass-first Ce/Mn, astatinated/radiohalogen words, an arbitrary `radiofutureword`, bare labelled/labeled/tagged and later labels after a negated label all refuse in the tested cases. `non-labelled radiofluorinated trastuzumab`, `non-radio-labelled astatinated trastuzumab`, `non-tagged labelled trastuzumab` and the reverse label order all have no map centre or QA evidence. An earlier negation does not hide the later positive label. Bare/non-labelled controls, `radiotherapy`, `radiology`, `radiofrequency`, ordinary doses/schedules, and CR/CO/SE/LA space exceptions retain their expected readings. [All 87 cases](attacks/new_summary.json), [30 checked refusals](attacks/assertions.json).
+
+**M23-S1 — new negation over-refusal, nonblocking:** `non-radio-astatinated trastuzumab` now refuses, while `non-radioastatinated trastuzumab` still draws the parent. HEAD^ accepts both. The negation loop only recognizes `_LABEL_WORDS`, `_LABELLED` and `_LABEL_STEMS` when extending the negated run (`src/query.py:218-224`); generic `radioastatinated` is recognized only later by the separate radio fallback. In the split spelling it marks only `radio` as negated, and the remaining `astatinated` triggers its own stem. The separate positive-label controls still refuse correctly. [Four-case current/previous stage replay](attacks/negation_results.json). This is a recall/negation-consistency issue, not a parent substitution blocker; it is not silently added to the user's accepted exceptions.
+
+The user-recorded M5-S2, rapamycin forms, `Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate`, `9-amino-20(s)-camptothecin`, `il-6ra`, `TAK-659F`, `GS-11CAL-101`, `3en3hg4wsw`, same-list-item over-refusal and ChEMBL bevacizumab development-code synonyms remain separately recorded and **do not determine the verdict**. Other historical residuals remain visible in the matrices; age alone does not make an unresolved observation accepted.

+"""Assemble this round's executed evidence; never write outside m23."""
+import json
+import posixpath
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def read(name):
+    return (OUT / name).read_text(encoding="utf8")
+
+
+def rebase(text, folder):
+    def sub(match):
+        target = match.group(1)
+        if re.match(r"^(?:[a-z]+:|/|#)", target):
+            return match.group(0)
+        return "](" + posixpath.normpath(folder + "/" + target) + ")"
+    return re.sub(r"\]\(([^\s)]+)\)", sub, text)
+
+
+history_path = OUT / "history/matrix.md"
+if not history_path.exists():
+    alternatives = list((OUT / "history").glob("*table*.md"))
+    if not alternatives:
+        raise FileNotFoundError("Awaiting current historical per-finding matrix")
+    history_path = alternatives[0]
+
+history = rebase(history_path.read_text(encoding="utf8"), "history")
+contract = rebase(read("contract/historical_table.md"), "contract")
+cost = rebase(read("cost/SUMMARY.md"), "cost")
+cost = re.sub(r"^#{1,2} .*\n", "", cost, count=1)
+standards = rebase(read("contract/STANDARDS.md"), "contract")
+standards = re.sub(r"^# .*\n", "", standards, count=1)
+
+text = read("report_core.md") + """
+
+## Earlier naming reproductions — FIXED / NOT FIXED
+
+The historical lane reran earlier scripts against current HEAD and retained all results, including the accepted exceptions and unaccepted diagnostics. Its provenance manifest distinguishes reused original tests, adapters and frozen coverage replays. The fresh **7,511-key alias sweep** has **zero new losses/recoveries versus M22**, with **71 raw-alias losses and 320 diagnostics**. The frozen **1,648-question** comparison records **68 identical / 1,556 metadata-only / 24 semantic differences / zero errors**; its classifications are unchanged from M22 and are not defect counts. The matrices are coverage entries and overlap, so their row totals are not summed into a unique-defect count. [Execution and coverage summary](history/SUMMARY.md), [script provenance](history/reuse_manifest.json).
+
+""" + history + """
+
+## Earlier graph, values, page, server and boundary reproductions
+
+The independent contract matrix has **41 rows: 36 FIXED / 5 NOT FIXED**. It deliberately retains the earlier nonblocking and diagnostic qualifications. In particular, old split-mass sentences containing `labelled` are now refused because of the new word rule; that is not proof of broader isotope-only parsing. All exact M22 blockers are **FIXED**. [Contract summary](contract/SUMMARY.md), [execution records](contract/execution.json), [historical callback assertions](contract/callback_assertions.json).
+

Each complete HTTP map and each complete evidence array equals the appropriate bare-trastuzumab result. Reversing the phrase, adding a trailing chelator (`DOTACe134DOTA trastuzumab`), using `DOTAMn52m`, and using Herceptin corroborate the same mechanism. [All-stage traces](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page rendering](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+Paired controls **`DOTA-Ce134 trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA134Ce trastuzumab`**, **`Ce134DOTA trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA52Mn trastuzumab`** and **`DOTA-Ce-134 trastuzumab`** all refuse. The issue is specifically the position of a symbol-first pair inside a word; the exact M22 mass-first cases are fixed.
+
+**Cause:** the new symbol-first expression at `src/query.py:268` is `(?<![a-z])([a-z]{2})(\d{1,3})(?!\d)`. Its negative lookbehind rejects the `Ce134` part of `dotace134` because another letter precedes it. `Ce134DOTA` matches at the start. The corresponding mass-first expression at `src/query.py:264-267` does allow preceding letters. Ce/Mn exist in `_NAME_MASS` but not the older `_MEDICAL_MASS` substring scanner (`src/query.py:124-136,144-158`); the neighbour/edge fallback uses an element set that also lacks them (`src/textrules.py:290-294`). Thus no later stage rescues the missed pair. The audit records the regex matches and actual stage outcomes, rather than inferring the failure solely from the source. [Assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [independent second probe](history/spec_probe.json).
+
+**Identity evidence:** Bailey et al. prepared and imaged `[134Ce]Ce-DOTA-Trastuzumab`. The compact audit input `DOTACe134` is a reviewer-generated notation variant of that labelled material, **not** a verbatim published name. The primary study supports the distinction from unmodified trastuzumab; it does not justify attaching the originator's graph evidence to the conjugate. [Primary study, DOI 10.1016/j.nucmedbio.2022.04.007](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0969805122000373).
+
+Required result: refuse the modified-product description or resolve the correct distinct product. A regression check should pair symbol-first **start / interior / end** positions with mass-first controls and the documented ordinary-text exceptions. No application repair was made in this audit.
+
+## M23-B2 — explicit nonradioactive conjugates still inherit the originator graph (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `trastuzumab conjugated to IRDye800CW` and `trastuzumab conjugated to DM1`.** Both pass every refusal stage and produce the complete bare-trastuzumab map, **13 map trials / 12 trial QA rows / 12 sponsor QA rows**, over original CLI HTTP and actual page callbacks. `IRDye800CW-conjugated trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab IRDye800CW conjugate`, `trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1`, `trastuzumab DM1 conjugate` and Herceptin variants corroborate the same missing product-descriptor guard. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [full-equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+This groups the optical-dye and cytotoxic-payload examples as **one descriptor-coverage mechanism**, not two independently counted code defects. M22 already recorded related IRDye/DM1/ADC strings as unadjudicated diagnostics. This round supplies explicit conjugation inputs and primary identity evidence; these were never user-accepted exceptions.
+
+**Identity evidence:** Jeong et al. describe covalently constructing a trastuzumab–IRDye800CW optical imaging probe and evaluating the resulting conjugate. The query explicitly names that modification. [Primary study, DOI 10.5230/jgc.2021.21.e18](https://jgc-online.org/DOIx.php?id=10.5230%2Fjgc.2021.21.e18). The FDA's KADCYLA description identifies trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1 via MCC as an antibody-drug conjugate, and its dosing section explicitly distinguishes it from trastuzumab. A phrase saying only “conjugated to DM1” need not uniquely identify that approved linker/product, but it still cannot designate the unconjugated parent. [FDA prescribing information, sections 11 and 2.2](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/label/2026/125427s125lbl.pdf). These are identity premises, not treatment recommendations or claims of clinical benefit. [Source notes and limits](attacks/sources.json).
+
+**Cause:** `RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` covers biosimilar/reformulation descriptors but no conjugated/covalently-linked payload construction (`src/textrules.py:139-152,202-212`). `_qualified` delegates to those product/tracer predicates (`src/query.py:315-337`). The new label check recognizes the enumerated radioactive stems and exact words `labelled`, `labeled`, `labelling`, `labeling`, `tagged`, but not these nonradioactive conjugation descriptions (`src/query.py:178-199,227-236`). The resolver yields CONTAINED_ALIAS plus UNRESOLVED residue; `_resolver_blocked` remains empty, `_marked` returns None, and the parent is emitted at `src/query.py:662-679`.
+
+Controls `IRDye800CW-labelled trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab tagged with IRDye800CW` correctly refuse. Merely replacing “labelled” by the equally explicit “conjugated to” loses the protection. `trastuzumab-DM1`, `ado-trastuzumab emtansine` and `Kadcyla` do **not** draw the parent in the exercised standalone calls, so they are not counted as leaking controls. The latter two are ambiguous in the current dictionary. No automatic choice of a specific ADC is required to fix this failure: refusal is sufficient.
+

+## Repair controls and should-fix observations
+
+The new mass-first Mn parser recognizes `52Mn` and `52mMn`; compact mass-first Ce/Mn, astatinated/radiohalogen words, an arbitrary `radiofutureword`, bare labelled/labeled/tagged and later labels after a negated label all refuse in the tested cases. `non-labelled radiofluorinated trastuzumab`, `non-radio-labelled astatinated trastuzumab`, `non-tagged labelled trastuzumab` and the reverse label order all have no map centre or QA evidence. [All 87 cases](attacks/new_summary.json), [30 checked refusals](attacks/assertions.json).
+
+**M23-S1 — new negation over-refusal, nonblocking:** `non-radio-astatinated trastuzumab` now refuses, while `non-radioastatinated trastuzumab` still draws the parent. HEAD^ accepts both. The negation loop only recognizes `_LABEL_WORDS`, `_LABELLED` and `_LABEL_STEMS` when extending the negated run (`src/query.py:218-224`); generic `radioastatinated` is recognized only later by the separate radio fallback. In the split spelling it marks only `radio` as negated, and the remaining `astatinated` triggers its own stem. The separate positive-label controls still refuse correctly. [Four-case current/previous stage replay](attacks/negation_results.json). This is a recall/negation-consistency issue, not a parent substitution blocker; it is not silently added to the user's accepted exceptions.

+"""Assemble this round's executed evidence; never write outside m23."""
+import json
+import posixpath
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def read(name):
+    return (OUT / name).read_text(encoding="utf8")
+
+
+def rebase(text, folder):
+    def sub(match):
+        target = match.group(1)
+        if re.match(r"^(?:[a-z]+:|/|#)", target):
+            return match.group(0)
+        return "](" + posixpath.normpath(folder + "/" + target) + ")"
+    return re.sub(r"\]\(([^\s)]+)\)", sub, text)
+
+
+history_path = OUT / "history/matrix.md"
+if not history_path.exists():
+    alternatives = list((OUT / "history").glob("*table*.md"))
+    if not alternatives:
+        raise FileNotFoundError("Awaiting current historical per-finding matrix")
+    history_path = alternatives[0]
+
+history = rebase(history_path.read_text(encoding="utf8"), "history")
+contract = rebase(read("contract/historical_table.md"), "contract")
+cost = rebase(read("cost/SUMMARY.md"), "cost")
+cost = re.sub(r"^#{1,2} .*\n", "", cost, count=1)
+standards = rebase(read("contract/STANDARDS.md"), "contract")
+standards = re.sub(r"^# .*\n", "", standards, count=1)
+
+text = read("report_core.md") + """
+

All reproduction executions exited zero. The fresh **7,511-key alias sweep** has **zero new losses/recoveries versus M22**, with **71 raw-alias losses and 320 diagnostics**. The frozen **1,648-question** comparison records **68 identical / 1,556 metadata-only / 24 semantic differences / zero errors**; its classifications are unchanged from M22 and are not defect counts. The matrices are coverage entries and overlap, so their row totals are not summed into a unique-defect count. +# Independent relation-map review — round 23
+
+Reviewed **`a7d5f7ff65ff4b3634908402a8a4432a4b1353b6`** on 2026-11-04 (Asia/Seoul). **CHANGES REQUIRED.** The three exact M22 blockers are fixed, but two blocking mechanisms remain: an embedded **symbol-first** isotope still escapes all refusal stages, and explicit optical-dye / cytotoxic-payload conjugate descriptions still draw the unmodified antibody. Both reproduce through the original CLI HTTP server and the actual page callbacks. The new positive leaks also existed with HEAD^; they are incomplete coverage, not newly introduced positive-link regressions.
+
+No tracked or application file was changed. New scripts, temporary files and outputs are confined to `audit_evidence_claims/m23/`. The pre-existing brief/log were preserved. The user-recorded over-refusals and ChEMBL bevacizumab synonym limitation do not determine this verdict.
+
+## Method and independently checked claims
+
+Repair notes were treated as hypotheses. Historical **FIXED / NOT FIXED** labels below apply to each exact reproduction, not every spelling of its defect class. Earlier scripts were reused with provenance and output-path adaptations; M2's partial work and completed M2b are included. A newly introduced refusal can close a reproduction without repairing its underlying isotope parser, and those cases are explicitly distinguished.
+
+- **350 tests passed in 43.12 s**, exit 0. The requested pytest command was run with bytecode disabled and `--basetemp` inside this round. [Output](contract/pytest.txt), [command/environment](contract/pytest_summary.json).
+- **6,469 live centres / 22 relation-direction-type-role combinations / zero independent-oracle errors**. **19 DB assertions, 24 UI assertions and four additional self-loop/unknown-type/retirement checks** pass. [Oracle](contract/db_validate.json), [DB assertions](contract/db_assertions.json), [UI assertions](contract/ui_assertions.json), [extra fixtures](contract/self_loop_unknown.json).
+- **36/36 ordinary demos** agree between direct Engine and original CLI HTTP; actual JavaScript callbacks pass the 36 examples and six map examples. [HTTP](contract/demo_http.json), [prior comparison](contract/demo_previous_comparison.json), [callbacks](contract/ui_m3_checks.json).
+- The new attack suite has **87 current/HEAD^ case entries** plus **four supplemental negation entries** (overlapping inputs are not summed as unique cases). Its **348 original CLI HTTP requests all return 200**. Assertions independently establish **17 leaking variants**, including complete map equality and both complete QA evidence-array equalities with the corresponding bare alias; **30 refusal controls** have no centre and zero QA evidence. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [negation supplement](attacks/negation_results.json).
+- The actual form callback renders all **87 HTTP map captures** without render errors. The blocker inputs produce a parent SVG and `CHEMBL1201585` trail. [Page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+The page harness runs the actual JavaScript in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. It tests generated markup, callbacks and state, not browser painting or browser-executed XSS. HEAD^ comparisons replace only `query.py` against the current other modules/data, not an entire historical deployment. Cost numbers are host observations. No model was called.
+
+## M23-B1 — symbol-first isotope inside a larger word still draws the parent (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `DOTACe134 trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab DOTAMn52`.** Both return **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, with **13 map trials**, **12 trial QA rows** and **12 sponsor QA rows**. Each complete HTTP map and each complete evidence array equals the appropriate bare-trastuzumab result. Reversing the phrase, adding a trailing chelator (`DOTACe134DOTA trastuzumab`), using `DOTAMn52m`, and using Herceptin corroborate the same mechanism. [All-stage traces](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page rendering](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+Paired controls **`DOTA-Ce134 trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA134Ce trastuzumab`**, **`Ce134DOTA trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA52Mn trastuzumab`** and **`DOTA-Ce-134 trastuzumab`** all refuse. The issue is specifically the position of a symbol-first pair inside a word; the exact M22 mass-first cases are fixed.
+
+**Cause:** the new symbol-first expression at `src/query.py:268` is `(?<![a-z])([a-z]{2})(\d{1,3})(?!\d)`. Its negative lookbehind rejects the `Ce134` part of `dotace134` because another letter precedes it. `Ce134DOTA` matches at the start. The corresponding mass-first expression at `src/query.py:264-267` does allow preceding letters. Ce/Mn exist in `_NAME_MASS` but not the older `_MEDICAL_MASS` substring scanner (`src/query.py:124-136,144-158`); the neighbour/edge fallback uses an element set that also lacks them (`src/textrules.py:290-294`). Thus no later stage rescues the missed pair. The audit records the regex matches and actual stage outcomes, rather than inferring the failure solely from the source. [Assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [independent second probe](history/spec_probe.json).
+
+**Identity evidence:** Bailey et al. prepared and imaged `[134Ce]Ce-DOTA-Trastuzumab`. The compact audit input `DOTACe134` is a reviewer-generated notation variant of that labelled material, **not** a verbatim published name. The primary study supports the distinction from unmodified trastuzumab; it does not justify attaching the originator's graph evidence to the conjugate. [Primary study, DOI 10.1016/j.nucmedbio.2022.04.007](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0969805122000373).
+
+Required result: refuse the modified-product description or resolve the correct distinct product. A regression check should pair symbol-first **start / interior / end** positions with mass-first controls and the documented ordinary-text exceptions. No application repair was made in this audit.
+
+## M23-B2 — explicit nonradioactive conjugates still inherit the originator graph (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `trastuzumab conjugated to IRDye800CW` and `trastuzumab conjugated to DM1`.** Both pass every refusal stage and produce the complete bare-trastuzumab map, **13 map trials / 12 trial QA rows / 12 sponsor QA rows**, over original CLI HTTP and actual page callbacks. `IRDye800CW-conjugated trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab IRDye800CW conjugate`, `trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1`, `trastuzumab DM1 conjugate` and Herceptin variants corroborate the same missing product-descriptor guard. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [full-equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+This groups the optical-dye and cytotoxic-payload examples as **one descriptor-coverage mechanism**, not two independently counted code defects. M22 already recorded related IRDye/DM1/ADC strings as unadjudicated diagnostics. This round supplies explicit conjugation inputs and primary identity evidence; these were never user-accepted exceptions.
+
+**Identity evidence:** Jeong et al. describe covalently constructing a trastuzumab–IRDye800CW optical imaging probe and evaluating the resulting conjugate. The query explicitly names that modification. [Primary study, DOI 10.5230/jgc.2021.21.e18](https://jgc-online.org/DOIx.php?id=10.5230%2Fjgc.2021.21.e18). The FDA's KADCYLA description identifies trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1 via MCC as an antibody-drug conjugate, and its dosing section explicitly distinguishes it from trastuzumab. A phrase saying only “conjugated to DM1” need not uniquely identify that approved linker/product, but it still cannot designate the unconjugated parent. [FDA prescribing information, sections 11 and 2.2](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/label/2026/125427s125lbl.pdf). These are identity premises, not treatment recommendations or claims of clinical benefit. [Source notes and limits](attacks/sources.json).
+
+**Cause:** `RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` covers biosimilar/reformulation descriptors but no conjugated/covalently-linked payload construction (`src/textrules.py:139-152,202-212`). `_qualified` delegates to those product/tracer predicates (`src/query.py:315-337`). The new label check recognizes the enumerated radioactive stems and exact words `labelled`, `labeled`, `labelling`, `labeling`, `tagged`, but not these nonradioactive conjugation descriptions (`src/query.py:178-199,227-236`). The resolver yields CONTAINED_ALIAS plus UNRESOLVED residue; `_resolver_blocked` remains empty, `_marked` returns None, and the parent is emitted at `src/query.py:662-679`.
+
+Controls `IRDye800CW-labelled trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab tagged with IRDye800CW` correctly refuse. Merely replacing “labelled” by the equally explicit “conjugated to” loses the protection. `trastuzumab-DM1`, `ado-trastuzumab emtansine` and `Kadcyla` do **not** draw the parent in the exercised standalone calls, so they are not counted as leaking controls. The latter two are ambiguous in the current dictionary. No automatic choice of a specific ADC is required to fix this failure: refusal is sufficient.
+
+## Common execution trace
+
+For each principal blocker input, the actual current calls show:
+
+| Stage | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| Intervention resolver | CONTAINED_ALIAS + UNRESOLVED; no BLOCKED component |
+| `_resolver_blocked` | `{}` |
+| `_qualified` | `False` |
+| `_item_has_isotope` | `False` |
+| `_marked` | Called for the parent; returns `None` |
+| `_whole_name` | `None` |
+| Public linker | Drug CHEMBL1201585; no Refused entity |
+| `POST /api/explore` | Entire map equals the corresponding bare-alias map |
+| Trial/sponsor `POST /api/ask` | Entire evidence arrays equal the parent's, 12 rows each |
+| Actual page form callback | Parent SVG and CHEMBL1201585 trail; no refusal |
+
+The map selects the accepted hit at `src/app.py:88-95`. This is **not** a case where the map discards an existing Refused entity; every guard misses the qualification. Both blockers are inherited with HEAD^ and independently fail the original product-identity approval bar.
+
+## Repair controls and should-fix observations
+
+The new mass-first Mn parser recognizes `52Mn` and `52mMn`; compact mass-first Ce/Mn, astatinated/radiohalogen words, an arbitrary `radiofutureword`, bare labelled/labeled/tagged and later labels after a negated label all refuse in the tested cases. `non-labelled radiofluorinated trastuzumab`, `non-radio-labelled astatinated trastuzumab`, `non-tagged labelled trastuzumab` and the reverse label order all have no map centre or QA evidence. An earlier negation does not hide the later positive label. Bare/non-labelled controls, `radiotherapy`, `radiology`, `radiofrequency`, ordinary doses/schedules, and CR/CO/SE/LA space exceptions retain their expected readings. [All 87 cases](attacks/new_summary.json), [30 checked refusals](attacks/assertions.json).
+
+**M23-S1 — new negation over-refusal, nonblocking:** `non-radio-astatinated trastuzumab` now refuses, while `non-radioastatinated trastuzumab` still draws the parent. HEAD^ accepts both. The negation loop only recognizes `_LABEL_WORDS`, `_LABELLED` and `_LABEL_STEMS` when extending the negated run (`src/query.py:218-224`); generic `radioastatinated` is recognized only later by the separate radio fallback. In the split spelling it marks only `radio` as negated, and the remaining `astatinated` triggers its own stem. The separate positive-label controls still refuse correctly. [Four-case current/previous stage replay](attacks/negation_results.json). This is a recall/negation-consistency issue, not a parent substitution blocker; it is not silently added to the user's accepted exceptions.
+
+The exact earlier M21 ordinary control **`trastuzumab not radioactively labelled`** also regresses: the M22 rerun accepted it, while current HEAD refuses with no centre and zero QA evidence. Here the shortest negated run ends at `radioactively`; `labelled` then independently triggers the new word rule. This is retained as **NOT FIXED** in the historical matrix and grouped with the negation-consistency issue, not counted as another P1. [Fresh original-case stages](history/replay/m21_exact/new_results.json), [old/current comparison](history/m21_semantic_changes.json).
+
+The user-recorded M5-S2, rapamycin forms, `Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate`, `9-amino-20(s)-camptothecin`, `il-6ra`, `TAK-659F`, `GS-11CAL-101`, `3en3hg4wsw`, same-list-item over-refusal and ChEMBL bevacizumab development-code synonyms remain separately recorded and **do not determine the verdict**. Other historical residuals remain visible in the matrices; age alone does not make an unresolved observation accepted.

+# Independent relation-map review — round 23
+
+Reviewed **`a7d5f7ff65ff4b3634908402a8a4432a4b1353b6`** on 2026-11-04 (Asia/Seoul). **CHANGES REQUIRED.** The three exact M22 blockers are fixed, but two blocking mechanisms remain: an embedded **symbol-first** isotope still escapes all refusal stages, and explicit optical-dye / cytotoxic-payload conjugate descriptions still draw the unmodified antibody. Both reproduce through the original CLI HTTP server and the actual page callbacks. The new positive leaks also existed with HEAD^; they are incomplete coverage, not newly introduced positive-link regressions.
+
+An additional inherited AACT identity-data defect is disclosed below as M23-D1; it is separate from these two code-path mechanisms and is not a user-accepted exception. No tracked or application file was changed. New scripts, temporary files and outputs are confined to `audit_evidence_claims/m23/`. The pre-existing brief/log were preserved. The user-recorded over-refusals and ChEMBL bevacizumab synonym limitation do not determine this verdict.
+
+## Method and independently checked claims
+
+Repair notes were treated as hypotheses. Historical **FIXED / NOT FIXED** labels below apply to each exact reproduction, not every spelling of its defect class. Earlier scripts were reused with provenance and output-path adaptations; M2's partial work and completed M2b are included. A newly introduced refusal can close a reproduction without repairing its underlying isotope parser, and those cases are explicitly distinguished.
+
+- **350 tests passed in 43.12 s**, exit 0. The requested pytest command was run with bytecode disabled and `--basetemp` inside this round. [Output](contract/pytest.txt), [command/environment](contract/pytest_summary.json).
+- **6,469 live centres / 22 relation-direction-type-role combinations / zero independent-oracle errors**. **19 DB assertions, 24 UI assertions and four additional self-loop/unknown-type/retirement checks** pass. [Oracle](contract/db_validate.json), [DB assertions](contract/db_assertions.json), [UI assertions](contract/ui_assertions.json), [extra fixtures](contract/self_loop_unknown.json).
+- **36/36 ordinary demos** agree between direct Engine and original CLI HTTP; actual JavaScript callbacks pass the 36 examples and six map examples. [HTTP](contract/demo_http.json), [prior comparison](contract/demo_previous_comparison.json), [callbacks](contract/ui_m3_checks.json).
+- The new attack suite has **87 current/HEAD^ case entries** plus **four supplemental negation entries** (overlapping inputs are not summed as unique cases). Its **348 original CLI HTTP requests all return 200**. Assertions independently establish **17 leaking variants**, including complete map equality and both complete QA evidence-array equalities with the corresponding bare alias; **30 refusal controls** have no centre and zero QA evidence. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [negation supplement](attacks/negation_results.json).
+- The actual form callback renders all **87 HTTP map captures** without render errors. The blocker inputs produce a parent SVG and `CHEMBL1201585` trail. [Page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).

HEAD^ comparisons replace only `query.py` against the current other modules/data, not an entire historical deployment. Cost numbers are host observations. No model was called.
+
+## M23-B1 — symbol-first isotope inside a larger word still draws the parent (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `DOTACe134 trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab DOTAMn52`.** Both return **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, with **13 map trials**, **12 trial QA rows** and **12 sponsor QA rows**. Each complete HTTP map and each complete evidence array equals the appropriate bare-trastuzumab result. Reversing the phrase, adding a trailing chelator (`DOTACe134DOTA trastuzumab`), using `DOTAMn52m`, and using Herceptin corroborate the same mechanism. [All-stage traces](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page rendering](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+Paired controls **`DOTA-Ce134 trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA134Ce trastuzumab`**, **`Ce134DOTA trastuzumab`**, **`DOTA52Mn trastuzumab`** and **`DOTA-Ce-134 trastuzumab`** all refuse. The issue is specifically the position of a symbol-first pair inside a word; the exact M22 mass-first cases are fixed.
+
+**Cause:** the new symbol-first expression at `src/query.py:268` is `(?<![a-z])([a-z]{2})(\d{1,3})(?!\d)`. Its negative lookbehind rejects the `Ce134` part of `dotace134` because another letter precedes it. `Ce134DOTA` matches at the start. The corresponding mass-first expression at `src/query.py:264-267` does allow preceding letters. Ce/Mn exist in `_NAME_MASS` but not the older `_MEDICAL_MASS` substring scanner (`src/query.py:124-136,144-158`); the neighbour/edge fallback uses an element set that also lacks them (`src/textrules.py:290-294`). Thus no later stage rescues the missed pair. The audit records the regex matches and actual stage outcomes, rather than inferring the failure solely from the source. [Assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [independent second probe](history/spec_probe.json).
+
+**Identity evidence:** Bailey et al. prepared and imaged `[134Ce]Ce-DOTA-Trastuzumab`. The compact audit input `DOTACe134` is a reviewer-generated notation variant of that labelled material, **not** a verbatim published name. The primary study supports the distinction from unmodified trastuzumab; it does not justify attaching the originator's graph evidence to the conjugate. [Primary study, DOI 10.1016/j.nucmedbio.2022.04.007](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0969805122000373).
+
+Required result: refuse the modified-product description or resolve the correct distinct product. A regression check should pair symbol-first **start / interior / end** positions with mass-first controls and the documented ordinary-text exceptions. No application repair was made in this audit.
+
+## M23-B2 — explicit nonradioactive conjugates still inherit the originator graph (P1)
+
+**Exact inputs: `trastuzumab conjugated to IRDye800CW` and `trastuzumab conjugated to DM1`.** Both pass every refusal stage and produce the complete bare-trastuzumab map, **13 map trials / 12 trial QA rows / 12 sponsor QA rows**, over original CLI HTTP and actual page callbacks. `IRDye800CW-conjugated trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab IRDye800CW conjugate`, `trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1`, `trastuzumab DM1 conjugate` and Herceptin variants corroborate the same missing product-descriptor guard. [Stages](attacks/new_results.json), [HTTP](checks/independent_m23_http.json), [full-equality assertions](attacks/assertions.json), [page captures](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+This groups the optical-dye and cytotoxic-payload examples as **one descriptor-coverage mechanism**, not two independently counted code defects. M22 already recorded related IRDye/DM1/ADC strings as unadjudicated diagnostics. This round supplies explicit conjugation inputs and primary identity evidence; these were never user-accepted exceptions.
+
+**Identity evidence:** Jeong et al. describe covalently constructing a trastuzumab–IRDye800CW optical imaging probe and evaluating the resulting conjugate. The query explicitly names that modification. [Primary study, DOI 10.5230/jgc.2021.21.e18](https://jgc-online.org/DOIx.php?id=10.5230%2Fjgc.2021.21.e18). The FDA's KADCYLA description identifies trastuzumab covalently linked to DM1 via MCC as an antibody-drug conjugate, and its dosing section explicitly distinguishes it from trastuzumab. A phrase saying only “conjugated to DM1” need not uniquely identify that approved linker/product, but it still cannot designate the unconjugated parent. [FDA prescribing information, sections 11 and 2.2](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/label/2026/125427s125lbl.pdf). These are identity premises, not treatment recommendations or claims of clinical benefit. [Source notes and limits](attacks/sources.json).
+
+**Cause:** `RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` covers biosimilar/reformulation descriptors but no conjugated/covalently-linked payload construction (`src/textrules.py:139-152,202-212`). `_qualified` delegates to those product/tracer predicates (`src/query.py:315-337`). The new label check recognizes the enumerated radioactive stems and exact words `labelled`, `labeled`, `labelling`, `labeling`, `tagged`, but not these nonradioactive conjugation descriptions (`src/query.py:178-199,227-236`). The resolver yields CONTAINED_ALIAS plus UNRESOLVED residue; `_resolver_blocked` remains empty, `_marked` returns None, and the parent is emitted at `src/query.py:662-679`.
+
+Controls `IRDye800CW-labelled trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab tagged with IRDye800CW` correctly refuse. Merely replacing “labelled” by the equally explicit “conjugated to” loses the protection. `trastuzumab-DM1`, `ado-trastuzumab emtansine` and `Kadcyla` do **not** draw the parent in the exercised standalone calls, so they are not counted as leaking controls. The latter two are ambiguous in the current dictionary. No automatic choice of a specific ADC is required to fix this failure: refusal is sufficient.
+

+
+For each principal blocker input, the actual current calls show:
+
+| Stage | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| Intervention resolver | CONTAINED_ALIAS + UNRESOLVED; no BLOCKED component |
+| `_resolver_blocked` | `{}` |

+| Public linker | Drug CHEMBL1201585; no Refused entity |
+| `POST /api/explore` | Entire map equals the corresponding bare-alias map |
+| Trial/sponsor `POST /api/ask` | Entire evidence arrays equal the parent's, 12 rows each |
+| Actual page form callback | Parent SVG and CHEMBL1201585 trail; no refusal |
+
+The map selects the accepted hit at `src/app.py:88-95`. This is **not** a case where the map discards an existing Refused entity; every guard misses the qualification. Both blockers are inherited with HEAD^ and independently fail the original product-identity approval bar.

+## Repair controls and should-fix observations
+
+The new mass-first Mn parser recognizes `52Mn` and `52mMn`; compact mass-first Ce/Mn, astatinated/radiohalogen words, an arbitrary `radiofutureword`, bare labelled/labeled/tagged and later labels after a negated label all refuse in the tested cases. `non-labelled radiofluorinated trastuzumab`, `non-radio-labelled astatinated trastuzumab`, `non-tagged labelled trastuzumab` and the reverse label order all have no map centre or QA evidence. An earlier negation does not hide the later positive label. Bare/non-labelled controls, `radiotherapy`, `radiology`, `radiofrequency`, ordinary doses/schedules, and CR/CO/SE/LA space exceptions retain their expected readings. [All 87 cases](attacks/new_summary.json), [30 checked refusals](attacks/assertions.json).
+
+**M23-S1 — new negation over-refusal, nonblocking:** `non-radio-astatinated trastuzumab` now refuses, while `non-radioastatinated trastuzumab` still draws the parent. HEAD^ accepts both. The negation loop only recognizes `_LABEL_WORDS`, `_LABELLED` and `_LABEL_STEMS` when extending the negated run (`src/query.py:218-224`); generic `radioastatinated` is recognized only later by the separate radio fallback. In the split spelling it marks only `radio` as negated, and the remaining `astatinated` triggers its own stem. The separate positive-label controls still refuse correctly. [Four-case current/previous stage replay](attacks/negation_results.json). This is a recall/negation-consistency issue, not a parent substitution blocker; it is not silently added to the user's accepted exceptions.
+
+The exact earlier M21 ordinary control **`trastuzumab not radioactively labelled`** also regresses: the M22 rerun accepted it, while current HEAD refuses with no centre and zero QA evidence. Here the shortest negated run ends at `radioactively`; `labelled` then independently triggers the new word rule. This is retained as **NOT FIXED** in the historical matrix and grouped with the negation-consistency issue, not counted as another P1. [Fresh original-case stages](history/replay/m21_exact/new_results.json), [old/current comparison](history/m21_semantic_changes.json).
+
+**M23-D1 — inherited upstream identity-data defect, requiring separate curation:** the old chemistry control **`13-cis retinoic acid`** returns **CHEMBL38 / TRETINOIN / seven map trials** over current HTTP and the actual page. `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, `dictionary.index["13cisretinoicacid"]` and `dictionary.index["isotretinoin"]`, both contain an **AACT** synonym pointing to CHEMBL38; its preferred name is TRETINOIN. HEAD^ has the same mapping. [Exact dictionary subset, current/previous stages and HTTP](attacks/data_identity.json), [page capture](contract/ui_m23_http.json).
+
+NCI identifies 13-cis-retinoic acid as isotretinoin, while the FDA identifies tretinoin as all-trans-retinoic acid. Those names designate different geometric isomers. [NCI Drug Dictionary](https://www.cancer.gov/publications/dictionaries/cancer-drug/def/isotretinoin), [FDA RETIN-A MICRO description](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/label/2026/020475Orig1s029lbl.pdf). This was only a non-isotope control in `CODEX_MAP_M1_VERDICT.md:152,170`; the earlier “intended Drugs” wording at `audit_evidence_claims/m14/REPORT.md:179` and “preserve their identities” at `audit_evidence_claims/m15/REPORT.md:191` overstates its semantic correctness. Successful non-refusal of this input must not be reported as proof of correct identity. It is **not** the accepted ChEMBL/bevacizumab limitation, and no prior acceptance of this collision was found. It needs dictionary/source curation and downstream remeasurement; broadening an isotope-refusal rule is not the right repair. It is disclosed separately from the two code-path blockers, which already determine the verdict.
+
+The user-recorded M5-S2, rapamycin forms, `Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate`, `9-amino-20(s)-camptothecin`, `il-6ra`, `TAK-659F`, `GS-11CAL-101`, `3en3hg4wsw`, same-list-item over-refusal and ChEMBL bevacizumab development-code synonyms remain separately recorded and **do not determine the verdict**. Other historical residuals remain visible in the matrices; age alone does not make an unresolved observation accepted.
